                                                      \r\n  The setRef function in @radix-ui/react-compose-refs recursively triggers itself, causing React to hit the maximum update depth limit.               \r\n  In React 19, ref callback behavior changed - ref callbacks can now return cleanup functions, and React may call ref callbacks differently than in React 18. When setRef calls  \r\n  ref(value) at line 11, it triggers a re-render which invokes setRef again via the composed ref callback at line 22, creating an infinite loop.    \r\n\r\n\r\n### Expected behavior\r\n\r\n  Composed refs should work without causing infinite update loops in React 19.                                                                                                    \r\n\r\n### Reproducible example\r\n\r\nI have platejs plugins with radix popovers and radix tooltips on them. If I write fast inside plate, the page breaks. \r\nI\'m on a Macbook M1 Pro with 32 GB. Memoize the composed ref callback to prevent unnecessary re-invocations                                                                                                     \r\n  2. This may be causing the ref to be re-invoked on each render cycle. {'role': 'toolResult', 'toolCallId': 'chatcmpl-tool-82014d7782790fda', 'toolName': 'read', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': '# Issue #3963: [React 19] Unstable composed-ref identities in several primitives still cause "Maximum update depth exceeded" after the Slot fix (#3899)\n\nState: CLOSED\nState reason: COMPLETED\nAuthor: @nakisgr\nCreated: 2026-06-12T23:48:26Z\nUpdated: 2026-06-15T21:43:35Z\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963\n\n## Body\n\n### Summary\n\n#3899 fixed the React 19 infinite re-render loop in `Slot` by stabilizing its composed ref (released in `react-slot@1.2.5`, closing #3799). The same unstable-identity pattern still exists in several other packages\' own code, and under React 19\'s ref-cleanup semantics each of them reproduces the exact same `Maximum update depth exceeded` crash.\n\n### Mechanism\n\n`useComposedRefs` is `React.useCallback(composeRefs(...refs), refs)`. When any member of `refs` is an inline arrow function (or the composition is done with a bare `composeRefs(...)` call in render), the composed callback gets a new identity every render. React 19 then detaches and re-attaches the ref on every commit; any composed state-setter ref toggles its state `null -> node` each cycle, which schedules another render, which produces another new identity - an infinite loop that ends in `Maximum update depth exceeded`.\n\nTypical stack:\n\n```\ndispatchSetState\nsetRef            (@radix-ui/react-compose-refs)\nArray.map         (composeRefs)\nsetRef            (outer composition re-firing the inner one)\n```\n\n### Affected sites (verified against the latest published versions, June 2026)\n\n| Package | Site | Unstable member |\n|---|---|---|\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectContentImpl` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItemAlignedPosition` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItem` | `(node) => contentContext.itemRefCallback?.(node, value, disabled)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItemText` | `(node) => setItemTextNode(node)` and `(node) => contentContext.itemTextRefCallback?.(...)` |\n| `react-dropdown-menu@2.1.17` | `DropdownMenuTrigger` | render-inline `ref: composeRefs(forwardedRef, context.triggerRef)` |\n| `react-menu@2.1.17` | `MenuSubTrigger` | render-inline `ref: composeRefs(forwardedRef, subContext.onTriggerChange)` (composed directly with a state setter) |\n| `react-popper@1.3.0` | `PopperContent` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-focus-scope@1.1.9` | `FocusScope` | `(node) => setContainer(node)` |\n| `react-dismissable-layer@1.1.12` | `DismissableLayer` | `(node2) => setNode(node2)` |\n| `react-scroll-area@1.2.11` | `ScrollArea` / `Scrollbar` / `Thumb` | `(node) => setScrollArea(node)`, `(node) => setScrollbar(node)`, `(node) => scrollbarContext.onThumbChange(node)` |\n\n`react-dismissable-layer` deserves emphasis: it wraps every menu/popover/dialog content stack, so after patching the others it remained the residual loop driver in our app.\n\n### Real-world impact\n\nReact 19.2.3 SPA (Vite). Intermittent full-page crashes (caught by our ErrorBoundary) on any page with an open Select/DropdownMenu/Popover - a few percent of page loads in production, and frequent enough under parallel E2E load to fail CI on most runs. After stabilizing all the sites above (local `pnpm patch`es), the crash is gone across thousands of E2E test executions: before the patches we reproduced it reliably; after, zero occurrences.\n\n### Suggested fix (same principle as #3899)\n\n- Pass state setters directly: `useComposedRefs(forwardedRef, setContent)` - identical semantics, stable identity.\n- Wrap closures that capture per-render values in `useCallbackRef` before composing.\n- Hoist render-inline `composeRefs(...)` calls (dropdown-menu trigger, menu sub-trigger) into `useComposedRefs` above the JSX.\n\nWe run all of these as local patches today and they are mechanical; happy to turn them into a PR if useful.\n\n### Environment\n\n- React / react-dom 19.2.3\n- @radix-ui packages at the versions listed above (all latest stable as of June 12, 2026)\n- Reproduces in Chromium (Playwright) and real Chrome; intermittent, load/timing dependent\n\n## Comments (5)\n\n### @ioxua · 2026-06-15T16:40:17Z\n\ncan you share the patches? If you don\'t have time I\'ll try to tackle it when I can.\n\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963#issuecomment-4711730862\n\n### @chaance · 2026-06-15T19:49:00Z\n\nActually @nakisgr this fix is very simple, I\'m just going to patch it myself today 👍\n\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963#issuecomment-4711820867\n\n### @nakisgr · 2026-06-15T19:58:38Z\n\n@chaance I just made the [PR](https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/pull/3967)\n\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963#issuecomment-4711908893'}], 'details': {'contentType': 'text/markdown', 'totalLines': 508, 'displayContent': {'text': '# Issue #3963: [React 19] Unstable composed-ref identities in several primitives still cause "Maximum update depth exceeded" after the Slot fix (#3899)\n\nState: CLOSED\nState reason: COMPLETED\nAuthor: @nakisgr\nCreated: 2026-06-12T23:48:26Z\nUpdated: 2026-06-15T21:43:35Z\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963\n\n## Body\n\n### Summary\n\n#3899 fixed the React 19 infinite re-render loop in `Slot` by stabilizing its composed ref (released in `react-slot@1.2.5`, closing #3799). The same unstable-identity pattern still exists in several other packages\' own code, and under React 19\'s ref-cleanup semantics each of them reproduces the exact same `Maximum update depth exceeded` crash.\n\n### Mechanism\n\n`useComposedRefs` is `React.useCallback(composeRefs(...refs), refs)`. When any member of `refs` is an inline arrow function (or the composition is done with a bare `composeRefs(...)` call in render), the composed callback gets a new identity every render. React 19 then detaches and re-attaches the ref on every commit; any composed state-setter ref toggles its state `null -> node` each cycle, which schedules another render, which produces another new identity - an infinite loop that ends in `Maximum update depth exceeded`.\n\nTypical stack:\n\n```\ndispatchSetState\nsetRef            (@radix-ui/react-compose-refs)\nArray.map         (composeRefs)\nsetRef            (outer composition re-firing the inner one)\n```\n\n### Affected sites (verified against the latest published versions, June 2026)\n\n| Package | Site | Unstable member |\n|---|---|---|\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectContentImpl` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItemAlignedPosition` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItem` | `(node) => contentContext.itemRefCallback?.(node, value, disabled)` |\n| `react-select@2.3.0` | `SelectItemText` | `(node) => setItemTextNode(node)` and `(node) => contentContext.itemTextRefCallback?.(...)` |\n| `react-dropdown-menu@2.1.17` | `DropdownMenuTrigger` | render-inline `ref: composeRefs(forwardedRef, context.triggerRef)` |\n| `react-menu@2.1.17` | `MenuSubTrigger` | render-inline `ref: composeRefs(forwardedRef, subContext.onTriggerChange)` (composed directly with a state setter) |\n| `react-popper@1.3.0` | `PopperContent` | `(node) => setContent(node)` |\n| `react-focus-scope@1.1.9` | `FocusScope` | `(node) => setContainer(node)` |\n| `react-dismissable-layer@1.1.12` | `DismissableLayer` | `(node2) => setNode(node2)` |\n| `react-scroll-area@1.2.11` | `ScrollArea` / `Scrollbar` / `Thumb` | `(node) => setScrollArea(node)`, `(node) => setScrollbar(node)`, `(node) => scrollbarContext.onThumbChange(node)` |\n\n`react-dismissable-layer` deserves emphasis: it wraps every menu/popover/dialog content stack, so after patching the others it remained the residual loop driver in our app.\n\n### Real-world impact\n\nReact 19.2.3 SPA (Vite). Intermittent full-page crashes (caught by our ErrorBoundary) on any page with an open Select/DropdownMenu/Popover - a few percent of page loads in production, and frequent enough under parallel E2E load to fail CI on most runs. After stabilizing all the sites above (local `pnpm patch`es), the crash is gone across thousands of E2E test executions: before the patches we reproduced it reliably; after, zero occurrences.\n\n### Suggested fix (same principle as #3899)\n\n- Pass state setters directly: `useComposedRefs(forwardedRef, setContent)` - identical semantics, stable identity.\n- Wrap closures that capture per-render values in `useCallbackRef` before composing.\n- Hoist render-inline `composeRefs(...)` calls (dropdown-menu trigger, menu sub-trigger) into `useComposedRefs` above the JSX.\n\nWe run all of these as local patches today and they are mechanical; happy to turn them into a PR if useful.\n\n### Environment\n\n- React / react-dom 19.2.3\n- @radix-ui packages at the versions listed above (all latest stable as of June 12, 2026)\n- Reproduces in Chromium (Playwright) and real Chrome; intermittent, load/timing dependent\n\n## Comments (5)\n\n### @ioxua · 2026-06-15T16:40:17Z\n\ncan you share the patches? I\'ve been hunting this issue for quite a while now...\n\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/issues/3963#issuecomment-4710150668\n\n### @nakisgr · 2026-06-15T17:21:47Z\n\nSure @ioxua , we run these as pnpm patches. {'role': 'toolResult', 'toolCallId': 'chatcmpl-tool-8f92ad706e317640', 'toolName': 'read', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': "# Pull Request #3967: fix(react): stabilize composed ref identities to end React 19 render loop (#3963)\n\nState: MERGED\nDraft: false\nAuthor: @nakisgr\nBase: main\nHead: fix/composed-ref-stability-react19\nMerge state: UNKNOWN\nCreated: 2026-06-15T19:57:17Z\nUpdated: 2026-06-15T21:43:34Z\nURL: https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/pull/3967\n\n## Body\n\n## Summary\n\nFollows up on #3899, which stabilized Slot's composed ref to fix the React 19 infinite re-render loop. The same unstable-identity pattern still exists in several other packages' own code, and under React 19's ref-cleanup semantics each reproduces the same `Maximum update depth exceeded` crash.\n\n`useComposedRefs` is `React.useCallback(composeRefs(...refs), refs)`. When a member of `refs` is an inline arrow (or the composition is a bare `composeRefs(...)` in render), the composed callback gets a new identity every render. React 19 detaches/re-attaches the ref on every commit; any composed state-setter ref toggles `null -> node` each cycle, scheduling another render — an infinite loop.\n\n## Changes\n\nSame principle as #3899 — give the composed refs a stable identity:\n\n- **Pass state setters directly** (`useComposedRefs(forwardedRef, setX)`), matching existing usage like `select.tsx` `onTriggerChange`:\n  - `react-dismissable-layer`: `DismissableLayer` → `setNode`\n  - `react-focus-scope`: `FocusScope` → `setContainer`\n  - `react-popper`: `PopperContent` → `setContent`\n  - `react-scroll-area`: `ScrollArea` → `setScrollArea`, `Scrollbar` → `setScrollbar`, `Thumb` → `scrollbarContext.onThumbChange` (already stabilized via `useCallbackRef`)\n  - `react-select`: `SelectContentImpl` / `SelectItemAlignedPosition` → `setContent`, `SelectItemText` → `setItemTextNode`\n- **Hoist render-inline `composeRefs(...)` into `useComposedRefs`** above the JSX:\n  - `react-dropdown-menu`: `DropdownMenuTrigger`\n  - `react-menu`: `MenuSubTrigger` (composed directly with the `onTriggerChange` state setter)\n- **Wrap closures that capture per-render values in `useCallbackRef`** before composing (they take extra args, so can't be passed directly):\n  - `react-select`: `SelectItem` (`value`, `disabled`) and `SelectItemText` (`value`, `disabled`)\n\n## Notes\n\n- Changes are mechanical and behavior-preserving; identities are now stable across renders.\n- `react-dismissable-layer` is emphasized in #3963 as the residual loop driver, since it wraps every menu/popover/dialog content stack.\n\nCloses #3963.\n\n## Files (7)\n\n- packages/react/dismissable-layer/src/dismissable-layer.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/dropdown-menu/src/dropdown-menu.tsx [CHANGED] (+3 -2)\n- packages/react/focus-scope/src/focus-scope.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/menu/src/menu.tsx [CHANGED] (+4 -2)\n- packages/react/popper/src/popper.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/scroll-area/src/scroll-area.tsx [CHANGED] (+3 -5)\n- packages/react/select/src/select.tsx [CHANGED] (+10 -6)\n\n## Comments (1)\n\n### @changeset-bot · 2026-06-15T19:57:23Z\n\n###  ⚠️  No Changeset found\n\nLatest commit: 20c8435a8f3ae5b62d65e822695d6fbe63832dc3\n\nMerging this PR will not cause a version bump for any packages. React 19 detaches/re-attaches the ref on every commit; any composed state-setter ref toggles `null -> node` each cycle, scheduling another render — an infinite loop.\n\n## Changes\n\nSame principle as #3899 — give the composed refs a stable identity:\n\n- **Pass state setters directly** (`useComposedRefs(forwardedRef, setX)`), matching existing usage like `select.tsx` `onTriggerChange`:\n  - `react-dismissable-layer`: `DismissableLayer` → `setNode`\n  - `react-focus-scope`: `FocusScope` → `setContainer`\n  - `react-popper`: `PopperContent` → `setContent`\n  - `react-scroll-area`: `ScrollArea` → `setScrollArea`, `Scrollbar` → `setScrollbar`, `Thumb` → `scrollbarContext.onThumbChange` (already stabilized via `useCallbackRef`)\n  - `react-select`: `SelectContentImpl` / `SelectItemAlignedPosition` → `setContent`, `SelectItemText` → `setItemTextNode`\n- **Hoist render-inline `composeRefs(...)` into `useComposedRefs`** above the JSX:\n  - `react-dropdown-menu`: `DropdownMenuTrigger`\n  - `react-menu`: `MenuSubTrigger` (composed directly with the `onTriggerChange` state setter)\n- **Wrap closures that capture per-render values in `useCallbackRef`** before composing (they take extra args, so can't be passed directly):\n  - `react-select`: `SelectItem` (`value`, `disabled`) and `SelectItemText` (`value`, `disabled`)\n\n## Notes\n\n- Changes are mechanical and behavior-preserving; identities are now stable across renders.\n- `react-dismissable-layer` is emphasized in #3963 as the residual loop driver, since it wraps every menu/popover/dialog content stack.\n\nCloses #3963.\n\n## Files (7)\n\n- packages/react/dismissable-layer/src/dismissable-layer.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/dropdown-menu/src/dropdown-menu.tsx [CHANGED] (+3 -2)\n- packages/react/focus-scope/src/focus-scope.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/menu/src/menu.tsx [CHANGED] (+4 -2)\n- packages/react/popper/src/popper.tsx [CHANGED] (+1 -1)\n- packages/react/scroll-area/src/scroll-area.tsx [CHANGED] (+3 -5)\n- packages/react/select/src/select.tsx [CHANGED] (+10 -6)\n\n## Comments (1)\n\n### @changeset-bot · 2026-06-15T19:57:23Z\n\n###  ⚠️  No Changeset found\n\nLatest commit: 20c8435a8f3ae5b62d65e822695d6fbe63832dc3\n\nMerging this PR will not cause a version bump for any packages.
